    .anchor.before {
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      transform: translateX(-100%);
      padding-right: 4px;
    }
    .anchor.after {
      display: inline-block;
      padding-left: 4px;
    }
    h1 .anchor svg,
    h2 .anchor svg,
    h3 .anchor svg,
    h4 .anchor svg,
    h5 .anchor svg,
    h6 .anchor svg {
      visibility: hidden;
    }
    h1:hover .anchor svg,
    h2:hover .anchor svg,
    h3:hover .anchor svg,
    h4:hover .anchor svg,
    h5:hover .anchor svg,
    h6:hover .anchor svg,
    h1 .anchor:focus svg,
    h2 .anchor:focus svg,
    h3 .anchor:focus svg,
    h4 .anchor:focus svg,
    h5 .anchor:focus svg,
    h6 .anchor:focus svg {
      visibility: visible;
    }
  </style><script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
      var hash = window.decodeURI(location.hash.replace('#', ''))
      if (hash !== '') {
        var element = document.getElementById(hash)
        if (element) {
          var scrollTop = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || document.body.scrollTop
          var clientTop = document.documentElement.clientTop || document.body.clientTop || 0
          var offset = element.getBoundingClientRect().top + scrollTop - clientTop
          // Wait for the browser to finish rendering before scrolling.
          setTimeout((function() {
            window.scrollTo(0, offset - 0)
          }), 0)
        }
      }
    })
  </script><script src="https://web.cmp.usercentrics.eu/modules/autoblocker.js"></script><script id="gtag-consent">

void function() {
  var STORAGE_KEY = 'theme-mode';
  var EVENT_NAME = 'theme-modechange';

  function isExplicit(v) { return v === 'light' || v === 'dark'; }

  function getSystemTheme() {
    try { return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light'; }
    catch(e) { return 'light'; }
  }

  function resolve(mode) { return isExplicit(mode) ? mode : getSystemTheme(); }

  function setTheme(mode) {
    var resolved = resolve(mode);
    document.body.classList.remove('light-mode', 'dark-mode');
    document.body.classList.add(resolved + '-mode');
    document.body.dataset.themeMode = mode;
    window.__themeMode = mode;
    window.__resolvedTheme = resolved;
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(EVENT_NAME, { detail: { mode: mode, resolved: resolved } }));
  }

  function persist(mode) {
    try {
      if (isExplicit(mode)) localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, mode);
      else localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  window.__setThemeMode = function(mode) { persist(mode); setTheme(mode); };

  var stored = null;
  try { stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY); } catch(e) {}
  var mode = (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' || stored === 'system') ? stored : 'system';

  setTheme(mode);

  try {
    window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').addEventListener('change', function() {
      if (!isExplicit(window.__themeMode)) setTheme(window.__themeMode);
    });
  } catch(e) {}
}();

hover:underline">Terms of Service</a></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div>
<p>How cool would it be to track large datasets and machine learning models
alongside your code, sidestepping all the limitations of storing it in Git?
Imagine cloning a repository and immediately seeing your datasets, checkpoints
and models staged in your workspace. Imagine switching to a different version of
a 100Gb file in less than a second with a <code>git checkout</code>.</p>
<p><em>💫 DVC is your <em>&quot;Git for data&quot;</em>!</em></p>
<h2 id="installing-dvc" style="position:relative">Installing DVC<a href="#installing-dvc" class="anchor after" aria-label="installing dvc permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>First things first, let&#x27;s install DVC. The easiest way to install dvc is with
<a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline"><code>uv</code></a> or <a href="https://pipx.pypa.io/stable/" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline"><code>pipx</code></a>:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">uv</span> tool <span class="token function">install</span> dvc  <span class="token comment"># or,</span>
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">pipx</span> <span class="token function">install</span> dvc</span></code></pre></div>
<p>Once it&#x27;s installed, verify dvc is available by running:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">dvc</span> <span class="token parameter variable">--help</span></span></code></pre></div>
<p>If you see a help message listing dvc commands, you are good to continue!</p>
<div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--tip--4ce16"><span class="styles-module--iconFloat--e36e0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></span><div class="styles-module--content--f6de4 styles-module--hasIcon--03e6f">
<p>See the <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/install">Installation Guide</a> for more installation options.</p>
</div></div>
<h2 id="initializing-a-project" style="position:relative">Initializing a project<a href="#initializing-a-project" class="anchor after" aria-label="initializing a project permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>Before we begin, settle on a directory for this guide. Everything we will do
will be self contained there.</p>
<div id="expand-to-prepare-a-project" class="collapsableDiv"><a href="#expand-to-prepare-a-project" class="anchor after" aria-label="Expand to prepare a project."><svg width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9m9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6"></path></svg></a><div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--tip--4ce16"><button type="button" class="styles-module--trigger--5ae29" aria-expanded="false"><span class="styles-module--triggerInner--516af"><span class="styles-module--icon--839f4"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-settings" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9.671 4.136a2.34 2.34 0 0 1 4.659 0 2.34 2.34 0 0 0 3.319 1.915 2.34 2.34 0 0 1 2.33 4.033 2.34 2.34 0 0 0 0 3.831 2.34 2.34 0 0 1-2.33 4.033 2.34 2.34 0 0 0-3.319 1.915 2.34 2.34 0 0 1-4.659 0 2.34 2.34 0 0 0-3.32-1.915 2.34 2.34 0 0 1-2.33-4.033 2.34 2.34 0 0 0 0-3.831A2.34 2.34 0 0 1 6.35 6.051a2.34 2.34 0 0 0 3.319-1.915"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"></circle></svg></span><span class="styles-module--triggerLabel--e6a11">Expand to prepare a project.</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down styles-module--chevron--3554a" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path></svg></span></button><div class="styles-module--collapseBody--c7981"><div class="styles-module--collapseBodyInner--44366"><div class="styles-module--collapsibleContent--11881"><p>Imagine we want to build an ML project from scratch. Let&#x27;s start by creating a
Git repository:</p><div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">mkdir</span> example-get-started
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">cd</span> example-get-started
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git init</span></span></code></pre></div><div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--info--ca73d"><span class="styles-module--iconFloat--e36e0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg></span><div class="styles-module--content--f6de4 styles-module--hasIcon--03e6f">
<p>This directory name is used in our
<a href="https://github.com/treeverse/example-get-started" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline">example-get-started</a> repo.</p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div>
<p>Inside your chosen directory, we will use our current working directory as a
<span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">DVC project</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">DVC project</span></span>. Let&#x27;s initialize it by running <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/init"><code>dvc init</code></a> inside a Git
project:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc init</span></span></code></pre></div>
<p>A few <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/project-structure/internal-files">internal files</a> are created
that should be added to Git:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git status</span>
</span>Changes to be committed:
        new file:   .dvc/.gitignore
        new file:   .dvc/config
        ...
<span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git commit</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-m</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Initialize DVC&quot;</span></span></code></pre></div>
<p>Now you&#x27;re ready to DVC!</p>
<h2 id="tracking-data" style="position:relative">Tracking data<a href="#tracking-data" class="anchor after" aria-label="tracking data permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>Working inside an <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/start#initializing-a-project">initialized project</a>
directory, let&#x27;s pick a piece of data to work with. We&#x27;ll use an example
<code>data.xml</code> file, though any text or binary file (or directory) will do. Start by
running:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc get</span> https://github.com/treeverse/dataset-registry <span class="token punctuation">\</span>
          get-started/data.xml <span class="token parameter variable">-o</span> data/data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
<div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--info--ca73d"><span class="styles-module--iconFloat--e36e0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg></span><div class="styles-module--content--f6de4 styles-module--hasIcon--03e6f">
<p>We used <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/get"><code>dvc get</code></a> above to show how DVC can turn any Git repo into a &quot;<a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/example-scenarios/data-registry">data
registry</a>&quot;. <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/get"><code>dvc get</code></a> can download any file or directory tracked in a <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">DVC
repository</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">DVC
repository</span></span>.</p>
</div></div>
<p>Use <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/add"><code>dvc add</code></a> to start tracking the dataset file:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc add</span> data/data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
<p>DVC stores information about the added file in a special <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/project-structure/dvc-files"><code>.dvc</code></a> file named
<code>data/data.xml.dvc</code>. This small, human-readable metadata file acts as a
placeholder for the original data for the purpose of Git tracking.</p>
<p>Next, run the following commands to track changes in Git:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git add</span> data/data.xml.dvc data/.gitignore
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git commit</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-m</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Add raw data&quot;</span></span></code></pre></div>
<p>Now the <em>metadata about your data</em> is versioned alongside your source code,
while the original data file was added to <code>.gitignore</code>.</p>
<div id="add-click-to-get-a-peek-under-the-hood" class="collapsableDiv"><a href="#add-click-to-get-a-peek-under-the-hood" class="anchor after" aria-label="Expand to get a peek under the hood"><svg width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9m9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6"></path></svg></a><div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--tip--4ce16"><button type="button" class="styles-module--trigger--5ae29" aria-expanded="false"><span class="styles-module--triggerInner--516af"><span class="styles-module--icon--839f4"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></span><span class="styles-module--triggerLabel--e6a11">Expand to get a peek under the hood</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down styles-module--chevron--3554a" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path></svg></span></button><div class="styles-module--collapseBody--c7981"><div class="styles-module--collapseBodyInner--44366"><div class="styles-module--collapsibleContent--11881"><p><a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/add"><code>dvc add</code></a> moved the data to the project&#x27;s <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">cache</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">cache</span></span>, and
<span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">linked</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">linked</span></span> it back to the <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">workspace</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">workspace</span></span>. The <code>.dvc/cache</code> will
look like this:</p><div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="text"><pre class="language-text"><code class="language-text">.dvc/cache/files/md5
└── 22
    └── a1a2931c8370d3aeedd7183606fd7f</code></pre></div><p>The hash value of the <code>data.xml</code> file we just added (<code>22a1a29...</code>) determines
the cache path shown above. And if you check <code>data/data.xml.dvc</code>, you will find
it there too:</p><div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="yaml"><pre class="language-yaml"><code class="language-yaml"><span class="token key atrule">outs</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span>
  <span class="token punctuation">-</span> <span class="token key atrule">md5</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> 22a1a2931c8370d3aeedd7183606fd7f
    <span class="token key atrule">path</span><span class="token punctuation">:</span> data.xml</code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div>
<h2 id="storing-and-sharing" style="position:relative">Storing and sharing<a href="#storing-and-sharing" class="anchor after" aria-label="storing and sharing permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>You can upload DVC-tracked data to a variety of storage systems (remote or
local) referred to as <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/data-management/remote-storage">&quot;remotes&quot;</a>.
For simplicity, we will use a &quot;local remote&quot; for this guide, which is just a
directory in the local file system.</p>
<h3 id="configuring-a-remote" style="position:relative">Configuring a remote<a href="#configuring-a-remote" class="anchor after" aria-label="configuring a remote permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h3>
<p>Before pushing data to a remote we need to set it up using the <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/remote/add"><code>dvc remote add</code></a>
command:</p>
<div class="toggle styles-module--toggle--27996"><input id="tab-_R_4d6sl_-0" type="radio" name="toggle-_R_4d6sl_" checked=""/><label class="styles-module--tabHeading--00f2d" for="tab-_R_4d6sl_-0">Mac/Linux</label><div class="tab styles-module--tab--b8c0a">
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">mkdir</span> /tmp/dvcstore
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc remote add</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-d</span> myremote /tmp/dvcstore</span></code></pre></div>
</div><input id="tab-_R_4d6sl_-1" type="radio" name="toggle-_R_4d6sl_"/><label class="styles-module--tabHeading--00f2d" for="tab-_R_4d6sl_-1">Windows (Cmd)</label><div class="tab styles-module--tab--b8c0a">
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">mkdir</span> %TEMP%/dvcstore
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc remote add</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-d</span> myremote %TEMP%<span class="token punctuation">\</span>dvcstore</span></code></pre></div>
</div></div>
<div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--tip--4ce16"><span class="styles-module--iconFloat--e36e0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg></span><div class="styles-module--content--f6de4 styles-module--hasIcon--03e6f">
<p>DVC supports many remote <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/data-management/remote-storage#supported-storage-types">storage types</a>, including Amazon S3, NFS, SSH, Google
Drive, Azure Blob Storage, and HDFS.</p>
<p>An example for a common use case is configuring an <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/data-management/remote-storage/amazon-s3">Amazon S3</a> remote:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc remote add</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-d</span> storage s3://mybucket/dvcstore</span></code></pre></div>
<p>For this to work, you&#x27;ll need an AWS account and credentials set up to allow
access.</p>
<p>To learn more about storage remotes, see the <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/data-management/remote-storage">Remote Storage Guide</a>.</p>
</div></div>

<h3 id="uploading-data" style="position:relative">Uploading data<a href="#uploading-data" class="anchor after" aria-label="uploading data permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h3>
<p>Now that a storage remote was configured, run <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/push"><code>dvc push</code></a> to upload data:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc push</span></span></code></pre></div>
<div id="push-click-to-get-a-peek-under-the-hood" class="collapsableDiv"><a href="#push-click-to-get-a-peek-under-the-hood" class="anchor after" aria-label="Expand to get a peek under the hood"><svg width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9m9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6"></path></svg></a><div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--tip--4ce16"><button type="button" class="styles-module--trigger--5ae29" aria-expanded="false"><span class="styles-module--triggerInner--516af"><span class="styles-module--icon--839f4"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></span><span class="styles-module--triggerLabel--e6a11">Expand to get a peek under the hood</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down styles-module--chevron--3554a" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path></svg></span></button><div class="styles-module--collapseBody--c7981"><div class="styles-module--collapseBodyInner--44366"><div class="styles-module--collapsibleContent--11881"><p><a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/push"><code>dvc push</code></a> copied the data <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">cached</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">cached</span></span> locally to the remote storage we
set up earlier. The remote storage directory should look like this:</p><div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="text"><pre class="language-text"><code class="language-text">.../dvcstore/files/md5
└── 22
    └── a1a2931c8370d3aeedd7183606fd7f</code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div>
<p>Usually, we would also want to Git track any code changes that led to the data
change ( <code>git add</code>, <code>git commit</code> and <code>git push</code> ).</p>
<h3 id="retrieving-data" style="position:relative">Retrieving data<a href="#retrieving-data" class="anchor after" aria-label="retrieving data permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h3>
<p>Once DVC-tracked data and models are stored remotely, they can be downloaded
with <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/pull"><code>dvc pull</code></a> when needed (e.g. in other copies of this <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">project</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">project</span></span>).
Usually, we run it after <code>git pull</code> or <code>git clone</code>.</p>
<p>Let&#x27;s try this now:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc pull</span></span></code></pre></div>
<div id="expand-to-simulate-a-fresh-pull" class="collapsableDiv"><a href="#expand-to-simulate-a-fresh-pull" class="anchor after" aria-label="Expand to simulate a &quot;fresh pull&quot;"><svg width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9m9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6"></path></svg></a><div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--tip--4ce16"><button type="button" class="styles-module--trigger--5ae29" aria-expanded="false"><span class="styles-module--triggerInner--516af"><span class="styles-module--triggerLabel--e6a11">Expand to simulate a &quot;fresh pull&quot;</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down styles-module--chevron--3554a" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path></svg></span></button><div class="styles-module--collapseBody--c7981"><div class="styles-module--collapseBodyInner--44366"><div class="styles-module--collapsibleContent--11881"><p>After running <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/push"><code>dvc push</code></a> above, the <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/pull"><code>dvc pull</code></a> command afterwards was
short-circuited by DVC for efficiency. The project&#x27;s <code>data/data.xml</code> file, our
<span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">cache</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">cache</span></span> and the remote storage were all already in sync. We need to
empty the <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">cache</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">cache</span></span> and delete <code>data/data.xml</code> from our project if we
want to have DVC actually moving data around. Let&#x27;s do that now:</p><div class="toggle styles-module--toggle--27996"><input id="tab-_R_l5p6sl_-0" type="radio" name="toggle-_R_l5p6sl_" checked=""/><label class="styles-module--tabHeading--00f2d" for="tab-_R_l5p6sl_-0">Mac/Linux</label><div class="tab styles-module--tab--b8c0a">
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">rm</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-rf</span> .dvc/cache
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">rm</span> <span class="token parameter variable">-f</span> data/data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
</div><input id="tab-_R_l5p6sl_-1" type="radio" name="toggle-_R_l5p6sl_"/><label class="styles-module--tabHeading--00f2d" for="tab-_R_l5p6sl_-1">Windows (Cmd)</label><div class="tab styles-module--tab--b8c0a">
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">rmdir</span> .dvc<span class="token punctuation">\</span>cache
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">del</span> data<span class="token punctuation">\</span>data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
</div></div><p>Now we can run <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/pull"><code>dvc pull</code></a> to retrieve the data from the remote:</p><div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc pull</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div></div></div>
<div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--info--ca73d"><span class="styles-module--iconFloat--e36e0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg></span><div class="styles-module--content--f6de4 styles-module--hasIcon--03e6f">
<p>DVC is designed for Git-based data and model versioning in local data science
and ML projects. If your workflow is centered on data lakes, object storage, or
routinely syncing tens of thousands of files or more, consider
<a href="https://docs.lakefs.io/" rel="nofollow noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline">lakeFS</a> for data version control at infrastructure
scale.</p>
</div></div>
<h2 id="making-local-changes" style="position:relative">Making local changes<a href="#making-local-changes" class="anchor after" aria-label="making local changes permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>Next, let&#x27;s say we obtained more data from some external source. We will
simulate this by doubling the dataset contents:</p>
<div class="toggle styles-module--toggle--27996"><input id="tab-_R_696sl_-0" type="radio" name="toggle-_R_696sl_" checked=""/><label class="styles-module--tabHeading--00f2d" for="tab-_R_696sl_-0">Mac/Linux</label><div class="tab styles-module--tab--b8c0a">
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">cp</span> data/data.xml /tmp/data.xml
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">cat</span> /tmp/data.xml <span class="token operator">&gt;&gt;</span> data/data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
</div><input id="tab-_R_696sl_-1" type="radio" name="toggle-_R_696sl_"/><label class="styles-module--tabHeading--00f2d" for="tab-_R_696sl_-1">Windows (Cmd)</label><div class="tab styles-module--tab--b8c0a">
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">copy</span> data<span class="token punctuation">\</span>data.xml %TEMP%<span class="token punctuation">\</span>data.xml
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token command">type</span> %TEMP%<span class="token punctuation">\</span>data.xml <span class="token operator">&gt;&gt;</span> data<span class="token punctuation">\</span>data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
</div></div>
<p>After modifying the data, run <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/add"><code>dvc add</code></a> again to track the latest version:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc add</span> data/data.xml</span></code></pre></div>
<p>Now we can run <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/push"><code>dvc push</code></a> to upload the changes to the remote storage, followed
by a <code>git commit</code> to track them:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc push</span>
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git commit</span> data/data.xml.dvc <span class="token parameter variable">-m</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Dataset updates&quot;</span></span></code></pre></div>
<h2 id="switching-between-versions" style="position:relative">Switching between versions<a href="#switching-between-versions" class="anchor after" aria-label="switching between versions permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>A commonly used workflow is to use <code>git checkout</code> to switch to a branch or
checkout a specific <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/project-structure/dvc-files"><code>.dvc</code></a> file revision, followed by a <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/checkout"><code>dvc checkout</code></a> to sync
data into your <span class="styles-module--desktop--d0a59"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4de06">workspace</span></span><span class="styles-module--mobile--67ad7"><span class="styles-module--highlightedText--4808b" role="button" tabindex="0">workspace</span></span>:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git checkout</span> <span class="token operator">&lt;</span><span class="token punctuation">..</span>.<span class="token operator">&gt;</span>
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc checkout</span></span></code></pre></div>
<h2 id="return-to-a-previous-version-of-the-dataset" style="position:relative">Return to a previous version of the dataset<a href="#return-to-a-previous-version-of-the-dataset" class="anchor after" aria-label="return to a previous version of the dataset permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>Let&#x27;s go back to the original version of the data:</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git checkout</span> HEAD~1 data/data.xml.dvc
</span><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token dvc">dvc checkout</span></span></code></pre></div>
<p>Let&#x27;s commit it (no need to do <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/command-reference/push"><code>dvc push</code></a> this time since this original version
of the dataset was already saved):</p>
<div class="gatsby-highlight" data-language="cli"><pre class="language-cli"><code class="language-cli"><span class="token line"><span class="token input">$ </span><span class="token git">git commit</span> data/data.xml.dvc <span class="token parameter variable">-m</span> <span class="token string">&quot;Revert dataset updates&quot;</span></span></code></pre></div>
<div class="styles-module--callout--7f0fa styles-module--info--ca73d"><span class="styles-module--iconFloat--e36e0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-info" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 16v-4"></path><path d="M12 8h.01"></path></svg></span><div class="styles-module--content--f6de4 styles-module--hasIcon--03e6f">
<p>As you can see, DVC is technically not a version control system by itself! It
manipulates <a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/user-guide/project-structure/dvc-files"><code>.dvc</code></a> files, whose contents define the data file versions. Git is
already used to version your code, and now it can also version your data
alongside it.</p>
</div></div>
<h2 id="following-this-guide" style="position:relative">Following This Guide<a href="#following-this-guide" class="anchor after" aria-label="following this guide permalink"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" width="16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg>
</a></h2>
<p>To help you understand and use DVC better, consider the following three example
scenarios: <strong>data pipelines</strong>, <strong>experiment tracking</strong> and <strong>model management</strong>.
You may pick any to start learning about how DVC helps you &quot;solve&quot; that
scenario!</p>
<p>Choose a trail to jump into its first chapter:</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong><a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/start/data-pipelines/data-pipelines">Data Pipelines</a></strong> - Use DVC as a build system for reproducible, data driven
pipelines.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong><a class="no-underline text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline" href="/start/experiments/experiment-tracking">Experiment Management</a></strong> - Easily track your experiments and their progress
by only instrumenting your code, and collaborate on ML experiments like
software engineers do for code.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Model Registry</strong> - Use the DVC model registry to manage the lifecycle of
your models in an auditable way. Easily access your models and integrate your
model registry actions into CICD pipelines to follow GitOps best practices.</p>
</li>
</ul></div><div class="styles-module--navButtons--e00e1"><a class="styles-module--navButton--61859 styles-module--prevLink--9c47c" href="/install/ide-plugins"><span class="styles-module--navButtonTitle--75f05"><i class="styles-module--navButtonIcon--47d19 styles-module--prev--968d6"></i><span>IDE Plugins</span></span></a><a class="styles-module--navButton--61859 styles-module--nextLink--4d0ec" href="/start/data-pipelines"><span class="styles-module--navButtonTitle--75f05"><span>Data Pipelines</span><i class="styles-module--navButtonIcon--47d19 styles-module--next--bba08"></i></span></a></div></div><div class="styles-module--container--14753"><nav aria-label="On this page" class="styles-module--nav--fbb2c"><div><h5 class="styles-module--header--35abb">Content</h5><hr class="styles-module--separator--0b56b"/></div><div class="styles-module--contentBlock--c9e49"><div id="link-get-started-with-dvc"><a href="#get-started-with-dvc" class="styles-module--headingLink--b1bd2 link-with-focus">Get Started with DVC</a></div><div id="link-installing-dvc"><a href="#installing-dvc" href="#return-to-a-previous-version-of-the-dataset" class="styles-module--headingLink--b1bd2 link-with-focus">Return to a previous version of the dataset</a></div><div id="link-following-this-guide"><a href="#following-this-guide" class="styles-module--headingLink--b1bd2 link-with-focus">Following This Guide</a></div></div></nav><div
